- **text-encoding-forensics**: 两个系统对"看起来一模一样"的文件算出不同的哈希/校验值，或脚本读出来是乱码、首行多个看不见字符。讲清肉眼看不到但字节不同的三类元凶——BOM（UTF-8 的 EF BB BF）、行尾符（CRLF 与 LF）、编码（GBK/latin 非 UTF-8），并给出取证顺序：先用 hexdump/xxd 看首尾字节，再怀疑内容被改。核心反直觉：文本 diff 工具常常忽略行尾与 BOM，"diff 没差异却哈希不同"恰恰说明是不可见字节而非内容变了。触发词：哈希对不上、sha256 不一样、乱码、首行有看不见字符、Windows 和 Linux 同一文件、校验失败、checksum 不匹配、编码。 ([kumu314/Agent-Evals](https://github.com/kumu314/Agent-Evals/tree/HEAD/t2/skills/text-encoding-forensics) · ★1)

- **textbook-exercises**: 生成带 Bloom 层级标注的成套习题时用它，可依据已有 Word 文档、教材、讲义或笔记出题：示范例题（完整解题过程）、引导练习（带提示）、独立习题（附参考答案）三类，答案按学科档案逐题验证。触发语如"根据这份讲义出题"、"给这一章生成例题"。通常由 textbook-chapter 调用，也可单独运行。不适用于档案没有验证手段的题型，也不负责组卷。需与 textbook 系列其余 skill 装在同一 skills 目录下。 ([cabbage2000-lab/textbook-writer-skills](https://github.com/cabbage2000-lab/textbook-writer-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/textbook-exercises) · ★48)
- **textbook-figure-remap**: Extract individual figures from PDF textbooks on demand with deterministic matching and built-in quality checks. Use when writing or supplementing a note that needs an embedded anatomy, classification, algorithm, or imaging figure; when the user asks to extract a figure; or when a note's TODO or REF points to a figure not yet embedded. ([JustinChangTW/ai-skills-core](https://github.com/JustinChangTW/ai-skills-core/tree/HEAD/skills/03-research-knowledge/textbook-figure-remap))
- **textbook-grounding**: Orthogonally-integrated Hegelian syntopical analysis for SAQ/VIVA/concept grounding with systematic textbook citations. Implements thesis extraction → antithesis identification → abductive synthesis across multiple authoritative sources. Tensor-integrated with /m command: activates S×T×L synergies (textbook-grounding × pdf-search × qmd = 0.95). Triggers on requests for model SAQ responses, VIVA preparation, concept explanations requiring textbook evidence, or any PEX exam content needing systematic cross-reference validation. ([Zpankz/mcp-skillset](https://github.com/Zpankz/mcp-skillset/tree/HEAD/textbook-grounding) · ★7)
- **textbook-index**: Build and query a table-of-contents index of math and crypto textbooks to answer "which book and chapter proves X?". Use when a proof needs a standard lemma, when idea mining needs an external mathematical structure, or when adding books to references/textbooks. ([jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill](https://github.com/jiayaoqijia/cryptoskill/tree/HEAD/skills/dev-tools/acprk-textbook-index) · ★81)
- **textbook-init**: 动笔写教材前要先规划并创建工作目录时用它：规划单本教材项目目录或多教材工作区，可选 git 版本管理与 README 工作说明，建好后把产出交接给 textbook。触发语如"初始化教材项目"、"创建教材工作目录"、"规划教材工作区"、"建一个写教材的目录"。只搭目录骨架，不创建流水线文件（.progress.json、00-教材设计.md、术语表.md）。写正文用 textbook（不经 init 它也会自建默认目录），续写已有项目直接用 textbook。需与 textbook 系列其余 skill 装在同一 skills 目录下。 ([cabbage2000-lab/textbook-writer-skills](https://github.com/cabbage2000-lab/textbook-writer-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/textbook-init) · ★48)
- **textbook-learn**: Turn a technical book or PDF into an offline, visual learning page that teaches it as a COURSE — a single self-contained HTML file with a left table of contents, a whole-book chapter map / reading path, a few chapters broken down deeply (core ideas + the book's own figures downloaded locally + a step-by-step worked example), and per-chapter ACTIVE-RECALL QUIZZES plus a progress tracker that remembers what you've read and how you scored. Use this whenever the user wants to LEARN, STUDY, or "从入门到掌握" a specific BOOK or PDF/EPUB — e.g. "帮我学《动手学深度学习》/ 把这本 PDF 做成逐章学习页 + 测验 / I want to work through… ([shangjunyang1986/ai-learning-skills](https://github.com/shangjunyang1986/ai-learning-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/textbook-learn) · ★1)
